- **optical-crosslink-eval**: Decide whether a **free-space optical** inter-satellite or feeder link beats RF for a given hop. This is the one place where the two domains literally merge: a laser crosslink *is* an optical system doing communication. The diffraction-limited optical beam has staggering gain and a near-zero beamwidth — the benefit is enormous, but the pointing burden and weather sensitivity are the costs.
